\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 4. Branch Scope and Production Context\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 This branch covers 
\f0\b Voice / Singing
\f1\b0  in Song and Musical Theatre contexts. It clarifies sung-vocal evidence, lyric storytelling through song, vocal descriptor specificity, style/subtype caution, accompaniment context, self-tape assessability, vocal-health safety and inclusive voice handling.\
The revised branch outputs are intended to improve:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls1\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Song and MT sung-vocal report specificity.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Song acting/storytelling as lyric/story/communication through song.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Vocal descriptor precision for pitch, rhythm, tone, diction, phrasing, communication and interpretation.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Safety around no vocal-health diagnosis from tape.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Anti-bias handling for access needs, accent, speech difference, hearing difference, gender-diverse voice and resource limitations.\
\ls1\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Output non-regression for Song-only, MT, Acting, Dance and Hybrid contexts.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Current live production behaviour remains separate. TapeCoach is live, but these Voice / Singing refinements are 
\f0\b not production-shipped behaviour
\f1\b0  unless later implementation planning and rollout occur.\

\cf0 Additional output-spec assets:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls7\ilvl0
\f0\b \cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 VOICE-OS-T01\'96T31
\f1\b0  cover Song-only containment, MT Vocal visibility, Acting/Dance label non-regression, generic praise, poor audio, production polish bias, home-capture fairness, fixed-material advice, no-brief invention, short-cut overclaim, belt/mix overclaim, style overreach, accompaniment fairness, paid-resource bias, vocal-health diagnosis, safe strain-like wording, accent/speech-difference bias, hearing/access support, gender-diverse stereotyping, live-room/process overclaim, timestamp underproduction, next-take specificity, comparison mismatch, rendered timestamp mismatch, Step 2 invention and presentation-padding drift.\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls7\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Display checks cover Song/Voice labels, Vocal visibility, component/category wording, comparison-page score/label parity, rendered-versus-persisted timestamps, chronological order, timestamp specificity, next-take specificity and presentation-note discipline.\
\ls7\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Generic-feedback suppression covers phrases such as \'93strong vocal control\'94, \'93lovely tone\'94, \'93secure pitch\'94, \'93powerful belt\'94, \'93nice mix\'94, \'93strong storytelling\'94, \'93perfect song choice\'94, \'93vocal health sounds good\'94, \'93ready because of voice type\'94 and \'93clean audio as vocal merit\'94.\
\ls7\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 No-brief / false-specificity protections block invented song titles, role/world claims, universal cut-length rules, universal era rules and substitute song advice where material is fixed.\
\ls7\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Accessibility and anti-bias protections block access-deficit language, accent hierarchy, gendered voice assumptions, speech-difference deficit inference, body/appearance inference and resource-polish merit.\
\ls7\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Timestamp expectations remain within the current maximum of 8 and must not invent timestamps.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Current-production MT acting + song examples.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Generic report / comparison templates.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Supplied report-polish and report-quality implementation context.\
\ls8\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Voice maturity-audit confirmation that no patch is required.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Bridge QA can show cross-branch and MT-adjacent risks, such as label parity, timestamp rendering, generic praise, component split and comparison-page behaviour. It cannot prove Voice-only release readiness, revised Voice behaviour, frontend label correctness or score stability.\

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 15. Compact Branch Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 Voice / Singing is mature at research, rubric, revision, final-audit and output-specificity level and does not require a branch patch before cross-branch consolidation. The branch uses the VOICE prefix and preserves the full traceability set: VOICE-S001\'96VOICE-S052, VOICE-SYN-F01\'96F17, VOICE-AUDIT-I01\'96I20, VOICE-AUDIT-R01\'96R21, VOICE-REV-C01\'96C17, VOICE-REV-S01\'96S18, VOICE-FINAL-D01, VOICE-OS-R rule groups, VOICE-OS-T01\'96T31 and VOICE-OS-I01\'96I12.\
The strongest branch assets are its field-semantics clarification, descriptor spine, safety guardrails and output-specificity test pack. Voice / Singing clarifies that 
\f2\fs26 vocal
\f1\fs24  means sung-vocal evidence in Song and Musical Theatre, while Acting / Monologue and Dance retain their non-singing proxy meanings. Song remains vocal-centred but must include interpretive evidence where assessable: lyric intention, communication, phrase action, emotional arc, character or song situation, and acting-through-song. Formal frameworks supplied vocabulary for pitch, rhythm, tone, diction, phrasing, communication and interpretation, but external marks, grades and percentages remain excluded.\
The branch also separates audio/technical assessability from vocal merit, treats accompaniment and self-accompaniment as task/context-specific, blocks production polish and paid-resource access as talent evidence, preserves fixed-material policy, and suppresses generic vocal praise unless anchored to observable evidence. B4 closes the safety gap: no vocal-health diagnosis from tape. Strain-like, fatigued or hoarse sound may be described only as cautious observation. Access needs, disability, neurodivergence, hearing difference, accent, speech difference, gender-diverse voice, assistive technology and reasonable adjustments are non-deficit context.\
Residual limitations are explicit: belt, mix and registration evidence is thin; jazz, folk and broad commercial-pop descriptors are uneven; actor-musician evidence is partial; no Voice-only live outputs exist; frontend labels, comparison-page parity, rendered timestamp parity, score stability, implementation behaviour and rollout effects remain unverified. These do not block consolidation, but they do require later live-output QA and implementation regression before release confidence.\
Verified: source-to-output-spec traceability, revised baseline compatibility, final audit approval and output-specificity mapping. Not verified: live Voice-only reports, frontend category labels, comparison-page behaviour, PDF/export parity, rendered timestamp parity, score stability or production rollout.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
